# 
# Maidenhead Mayhem contest definition file for DXLog.net 
#
CONTESTNAME=Maidenhead Mayhem
#CONTESTWEB=https://website.com
#CONTESTRULES=https://website.com/rules

# Allowed bands in contest
BANDS=160;80;40;20;15;10

# Allowed modes
MODES=CW;SSB;RTTY;PSK31;PSK63;FT4;FT8

# Overlays
OVERLAY=---;Rookie

# Station categories
STATION=Fixed

# Mode categories 
CATEGORY_MODES=Mixed

# Operator categories
CATEGORIES=Single operator

# Power classes
CLASS=150W or lower

# Double QSO definition
DOUBLE_QSO=PER_BAND_MODE
MODE_GROUP=CW;PH;DG;DG;DG;DG;DG
DOUBLE_QSO_MODE_CHECK=PER_MODE_GROUP

# Check multiplier window setup
DISPLAY_MODE_GROUPING=CW:CW;PH:SSB;DG:RTTY+PSK31+PSK63+FT8+FT4

# Exchange entry and validity check
CFG_MULT=ON
CFG_MULT_MANDATORY=YES
CFG_MULT_ERRORTEXT=Exchange field: Enter your large square grid (e.g., JO or JO65)
CFG_MULT_RGX_CHECK=^[A-R]{2}([0-9]{2})?$

# Own multiplier counts
OWN_MULT_VALID=YES

# Scoring
POINTS_TYPE=CALC
